output.md
Task 6 is done and committed as 5517028. All 14 `CardioLogDraftTest` tests pass, and ktlint and detekt are clean.

**What changed**
- `CardioLogFields.kt` has the `CardioLogField` enum and the per-activity field table, in the frames' order. `CardioActivity.logFields` in `CardioActivity.kt` reads from it. I did not check the table against the backend's `CardioActivity.kt`, because that repo isn't in this checkout. It follows the field lists in the task.
- `CardioLogDraft.kt` has the draft and its `fresh` and `of` constructors.
- `CardioLogChecks.kt` is a new file under the allowed `CardioLog*.kt` collateral. It holds the duration and pace parsing and the range checks. The draft's size would otherwise trip detekt.
- `CardioLogDraftTest.kt` has the 14 named tests.

**Decisions to confirm**
- **Filename:** ktlint's filename rule rejected `CardioLogFields.kt`, since the file holds a single class named `CardioLogField`. The path is fixed by the task's `**Files:**` field, so I added `@file:Suppress("ktlint:standard:filename")` rather than rename it.
- **`toRequest()` on an unparseable duration:** it throws instead of sending 0. `canSubmit` is meant to guard against that.
- **Range and parse messages you didn't specify:** a pace of the wrong shape gives `use m:ss`. An out-of-range duration gives `between 0:01 and 24:00:00 h`. The other ranges follow your *between X and Y <unit>* pattern. Rides show cadence in `rpm`, and elevation shows `ft` on a miles workout.

**Commands run**
- RED: `./gradlew :shared:desktopTest --tests '*CardioLogDraftTest'` failed to compile with `Unresolved reference 'CardioLogDraft'`, as expected.
- `./gradlew :shared:ktlintFormat` ran and finished.
- GREEN: the same test command passed once the draft existed.
- Guard proof, broken state: I changed the rower cadence range from 10..60 to 1..60 and re-ran the tests. `rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange` and `anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft` failed, and the build failed.
- Guard proof, restored: I put the range back to 10..60.
- First `ktlintCheck` run: failed on the filename rule above, then passed after the suppression.
- Final run: `./gradlew :shared:ktlintCheck :shared:detekt :shared:desktopTest --tests '*CardioLogDraftTest'` gave BUILD SUCCESSFUL.

**Pin-before-refactor and old-vs-new diff:** these don't apply, since the task adds new code and refactors nothing.

**Commit:** the repo had no git identity, so I committed with a throwaway author, `claude <a@b.c>`. You may want to amend it.

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_POWER
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.number
+
+private const val MINUTE = 60
+private const val HOUR = 3600
+private const val DAY = 86400
+private const val MAX_DISTANCE_METRES = 1_000_000.0
+private val DURATION_SHAPE = Regex("""(\d+):([0-5]\d)(?::([0-5]\d))?""")
+private val PACE_SHAPE = Regex("""(\d+):([0-5]\d)""")
+
+/** `m:ss` under an hour, `h:mm:ss` from one up. */
+internal fun durationText(seconds: Int): String = if (seconds < HOUR) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)
+
+/** Whole seconds of `h:mm:ss` / `m:ss`, or null for any other shape. */
+internal fun parseDuration(text: String): Int? {
+    val (a, b, c) = DURATION_SHAPE.matchEntire(text.trim())?.destructured ?: return null
+    val first = a.toIntOrNull() ?: return null
+    return if (c.isEmpty()) first * MINUTE + b.toInt() else first * HOUR + b.toInt() * MINUTE + c.toInt()
+}
+
+internal fun parsePace(text: String): Int? {
+    val (a, b) = PACE_SHAPE.matchEntire(text.trim())?.destructured ?: return null
+    return a.toIntOrNull()?.let { it * MINUTE + b.toInt() }
+}
+
+private fun between(
+    low: String,
+    high: String,
+    unit: String,
+) = "between $low and $high $unit"
+
+/** The message for [field]'s [text] on [activity] in [units], or null when it is fine (or empty). */
+internal fun fieldError(
+    field: CardioLogField,
+    text: String,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): String? {
+    if (text.isBlank()) return null
+    val rower = activity == CardioActivity.ROWER
+    val imperial = units == DistanceUnit.MI
+    return when (field) {
+        DURATION -> {
+            val seconds = parseDuration(text)
+            when {
+                seconds == null -> "use h:mm:ss"
+                seconds !in 1..DAY -> between("0:01", "24:00:00", "h")
+                else -> null
+            }
+        }
+
+        DISTANCE -> {
+            val unit = if (rower) DistanceUnit.M else units
+            val max = MAX_DISTANCE_METRES / unit.metres
+            val value = text.trim().toDoubleOrNull()
+            val name =
+                if (rower) {
+                    "m"
+                } else if (imperial) {
+                    "mi"
+                } else {
+                    "km"
+                }
+            if (value == null || value <= 0 || value >= max) between("0", number(max), name) else null
+        }
+
+        AVG_PACE -> {
+            val (low, high) = if (rower) 60 to 600 else 60 to HOUR
+            val unit =
+                if (rower) {
+                    "/500 m"
+                } else if (imperial) {
+                    "/mi"
+                } else {
+                    "/km"
+                }
+            if (parsePace(text) == null) {
+                "use m:ss"
+            } else if (parsePace(text) !in low..high) {
+                between(clock(low), clock(high), unit)
+            } else {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+
+        AVG_SPEED -> {
+            val value = text.trim().toDoubleOrNull()
+            if (value == null || value < 0.1 || value > 120.0) between("0.1", "120", if (imperial) "mph" else "km/h") else null
+        }
+
+        else -> {
+            wholeNumberError(field, text, activity, units)
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private fun wholeNumberError(
+    field: CardioLogField,
+    text: String,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): String? {
+    val (range, unit) =
+        when (field) {
+            AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE -> {
+                (30..250) to "bpm"
+            }
+
+            CALORIES -> {
+                (0..20000) to "kcal"
+            }
+
+            ELEVATION_GAIN -> {
+                (0..20000) to if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m"
+            }
+
+            AVG_CADENCE -> {
+                when {
+                    activity == CardioActivity.ROWER -> (10..60) to "spm"
+                    activity.isRide -> (1..250) to "rpm"
+                    else -> (1..250) to "spm"
+                }
+            }
+
+            AVG_POWER -> {
+                (1..2500) to "W"
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                return null
+            }
+        }
+    return if (text.trim().toIntOrNull() in range) null else between("${range.first}", "${range.last}", unit)
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_POWER
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.EFFORT
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.NOTE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.number
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's state (L1–L6): the text typed per field, the effort chip, the note, and which fields
+ * were left (errors show only on those). Pure; the 400 ms debounce lives in the sheet. A blank pace or
+ * speed is [derived] and never sent (`derived-never-sent`).
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val visited: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            EFFORT -> effort?.toString().orEmpty()
+            NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field].orEmpty()
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.toIntOrNull())
+            NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(visited = visited + field)
+
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in visited) errors()[field] else null
+
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> {
+        val found = mutableMapOf<CardioLogField, String>()
+        activity.logFields.forEach { field ->
+            fieldError(field, text(field), activity, units)?.let { found[field] = it }
+        }
+        val avg = text(AVG_HEART_RATE).trim().toIntOrNull()
+        val max = text(MAX_HEART_RATE).trim().toIntOrNull()
+        if (MAX_HEART_RATE !in found && avg != null && max != null && max < avg) {
+            found[MAX_HEART_RATE] = "max heart rate is below average"
+        }
+        return found
+    }
+
+    val canSubmit: Boolean get() = parseDuration(text(DURATION)) != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (field !in activity.logFields || text(field).isNotBlank()) return null
+        val seconds = parseDuration(text(DURATION))
+        val distance = distance()
+        return when (field) {
+            AVG_PACE -> CardioFormat.derivedPace(seconds, distance, null, activity, units)?.let { clock(it) }
+            AVG_SPEED -> CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(seconds, distance, null, units)?.toString()
+            else -> null
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest {
+        fun typed(field: CardioLogField) = text(field).trim().takeIf { field in activity.logFields && it.isNotEmpty() }
+
+        fun whole(field: CardioLogField) = typed(field)?.toIntOrNull()
+        return CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = checkNotNull(parseDuration(text(DURATION))) { "a log needs a duration" },
+            distance = distance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = typed(AVG_PACE)?.let { parsePace(it) },
+            avgSpeed = typed(AVG_SPEED)?.toDoubleOrNull(),
+            avgHeartRate = whole(AVG_HEART_RATE),
+            maxHeartRate = whole(MAX_HEART_RATE),
+            calories = whole(CALORIES),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = whole(ELEVATION_GAIN),
+            avgCadence = whole(AVG_CADENCE),
+            avgPower = whole(AVG_POWER),
+        )
+    }
+
+    private fun distance(): CardioDistance? {
+        if (DISTANCE !in activity.logFields) return null
+        val value = text(DISTANCE).trim().toDoubleOrNull() ?: return null
+        return CardioDistance(value, if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units)
+    }
+
+    companion object {
+        /** A new draft; in progress, Duration is the clock's whole seconds since `startedAt` (`clock-from-started-at`). */
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val blank = CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val started = workout.startedAt
+            if (workout.status != WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS || started == null) return blank
+            val seconds = (now - started).inWholeSeconds.coerceIn(0, Int.MAX_VALUE.toLong()).toInt()
+            return blank.with(DURATION, durationText(seconds))
+        }
+
+        /** The saved log as text for *Edit log*; derived values stay derived. */
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val blank = CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val log = workout.log ?: return blank
+            val unit = if (workout.activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else workout.distanceUnit
+            val saved =
+                mapOf(
+                    DURATION to durationText(log.durationSeconds),
+                    DISTANCE to log.distance?.let { number(it.value * it.unit.metres / unit.metres) },
+                    AVG_PACE to log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { clock(it) },
+                    AVG_SPEED to log.avgSpeed?.toString(),
+                    ELEVATION_GAIN to log.elevationGain?.toString(),
+                    AVG_HEART_RATE to log.avgHeartRate?.toString(),
+                    MAX_HEART_RATE to log.maxHeartRate?.toString(),
+                    AVG_CADENCE to log.avgCadence?.toString(),
+                    AVG_POWER to log.avgPower?.toString(),
+                    CALORIES to log.calories?.toString(),
+                )
+            val texts = saved.mapNotNull { (field, text) -> text?.let { field to it } }.toMap()
+            return blank.copy(texts = texts, effort = log.effort)
+        }
+    }
+}

+@file:Suppress("ktlint:standard:filename")
+
+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_POWER
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.EFFORT
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.NOTE
+
+/** A field of the cardio log sheet (L1–L6); which of them an activity draws is [CardioActivity.logFields]. */
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+private val outdoorRun =
+    listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, ELEVATION_GAIN, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE)
+
+private val outdoorRide =
+    listOf(
+        DURATION,
+        DISTANCE,
+        AVG_SPEED,
+        ELEVATION_GAIN,
+        AVG_HEART_RATE,
+        MAX_HEART_RATE,
+        AVG_CADENCE,
+        AVG_POWER,
+        CALORIES,
+        EFFORT,
+        NOTE,
+    )
+
+internal fun logFieldsOf(activity: CardioActivity): List<CardioLogField> =
+    when (activity) {
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> {
+            outdoorRun
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            outdoorRide
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            outdoorRide - ELEVATION_GAIN
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> {
+            outdoorRun - ELEVATION_GAIN - AVG_CADENCE
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ROWER -> {
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, AVG_POWER, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE)
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> {
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE)
+        }
+    }

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertFalse
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.test.assertTrue
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        units: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "w",
+        scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 10, 16),
+        displayName = "Run",
+        notes = notes,
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = activity.kinds.first(),
+        distanceUnit = units,
+        log = log,
+    )
+
+    private fun fresh(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        units: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+    ) = CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(activity, units), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T19:00:00Z"))
+
+    @Test
+    fun eachActivityLogsItsOwnFieldsInFrameOrder() {
+        val run = listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, ELEVATION_GAIN, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES)
+        val tail = listOf(CardioLogField.EFFORT, CardioLogField.NOTE)
+        assertEquals(run + tail, CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields)
+        assertEquals(run - ELEVATION_GAIN - AVG_CADENCE + tail, CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN.logFields)
+        val ride = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_SPEED, ELEVATION_GAIN, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CardioLogField.AVG_POWER),
+            ride.take(8),
+        )
+        assertEquals(ride - ELEVATION_GAIN, CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE.logFields)
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, CardioLogField.AVG_POWER, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES) + tail,
+            CardioActivity.ROWER.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES) + tail,
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL.logFields,
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        assertEquals(1992, fresh().with(DURATION, "33:12").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, fresh().with(DURATION, "1:12:40").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        listOf("45", "1:75", "abc").forEach {
+            val draft = fresh().with(DURATION, it)
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", draft.errors()[DURATION], it)
+            assertFalse(draft.canSubmit)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anEmptyDurationHoldsThePrimary() {
+        val draft = fresh()
+        assertTrue(draft.errors().isEmpty())
+        assertFalse(draft.canSubmit)
+        assertTrue(draft.with(DURATION, "30:00").canSubmit)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val draft = fresh().with(DURATION, "33:12").with(DISTANCE, "5.02")
+        assertEquals("6:37", draft.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(draft.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM), draft.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aTypedPaceReplacesTheDerivedOne() {
+        val draft = fresh().with(DURATION, "33:12").with(DISTANCE, "5.02").with(AVG_PACE, "6:30")
+        assertNull(draft.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(390, draft.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val draft = fresh(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE).with(DURATION, "1:12:40").with(DISTANCE, "38.6")
+        assertEquals("31.9", draft.derived(AVG_SPEED))
+        assertNull(draft.toRequest().avgSpeed)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val draft = fresh(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(DURATION, "8:24").with(DISTANCE, "2000")
+        assertEquals("2:06", draft.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        val km = fresh(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(DURATION, "8:24").with(DISTANCE, "7120")
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(7120.0, DistanceUnit.M), km.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val draft = fresh().with(AVG_HEART_RATE, "148").with(MAX_HEART_RATE, "139")
+        assertEquals("max heart rate is below average", draft.errors()[MAX_HEART_RATE])
+        assertNull(draft.errors()[AVG_HEART_RATE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", fresh(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(AVG_CADENCE, "8").errors()[AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val draft = fresh(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(AVG_CADENCE, "8")
+        assertTrue(AVG_CADENCE in draft.errors())
+        assertNull(draft.shownError(AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", draft.left(AVG_CADENCE).shownError(AVG_CADENCE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val started = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:31:00.000Z")
+        val now = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T19:12:06.900Z")
+        val open = workout(status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS, startedAt = started)
+        assertEquals("41:06", CardioLogDraft.fresh(open, now).text(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("", CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(), now).text(DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val log =
+            CardioLog(
+                durationSeconds = 4360,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgHeartRate = 141,
+                effort = 7,
+                derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 869,
+            )
+        val draft = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(status = WorkoutStatus.FINISHED, log = log, notes = "windy"))
+        assertEquals("1:12:40", draft.text(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", draft.text(DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("141", draft.text(AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals(7, draft.effort)
+        assertEquals("windy", draft.note)
+        assertEquals("", draft.text(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", draft.derived(AVG_PACE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val request =
+            fresh()
+                .with(DURATION, "30:00")
+                .with(CALORIES, "300")
+                .copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+                .toRequest()
+        assertEquals(CardioLogRequest(durationSeconds = 1800, calories = 300, effort = 6), request)
+    }
+}

The log field table, `CardioLogDraft` and the 14 tests are in and committed as `a225d6a`. The 14 tests, ktlint and detekt are green.

**What changed**
- `CardioLogFields.kt` is new: the `CardioLogField` enum and the per-activity field lists in sheet order.
- `CardioActivity.logFields` now delegates to those lists.
- `CardioLogDraft.kt` is new: an immutable data class with the API from the task. Checks use the backend ranges, with rower cadence 10–60.
- Derived pace and speed come from `CardioFormat.derivedPace` and `derivedSpeed`, and `toRequest()` never sends them or the note.
- `CardioLogDraftTest.kt` is new, with the 14 named tests.

**Not checked**
- I did not diff the field lists against the backend's `CardioActivity.logFields` or `CardioLogSpecification`. They come from the task text only.
- The task's "L4's figures" for the rower test were not available. I used 7120 m in 29:54, which gives 2:06 per 500 m.

**Choices the task didn't pin down**
- The range messages for duration, distance, pace and speed are my own wording; only the cadence, max-heart-rate and duration-shape texts were specified:
  - Duration out of range: `between 0:01 and 24:00:00`.
  - Distance: `between 0 and <max> <unit>`.
  - Pace: `between 1:00 and <max> <unit>`.
  - Speed: `between 0.1 and 120 <unit>`.
- Pace is parsed as `m:ss` only.

**Commands**
- `./gradlew :shared:ktlintFormat` failed on the first run with style violations. I fixed them by hand and ran `ktlintFormat` and `ktlintCheck` again, which passed.
- `./gradlew :shared:desktopTest --tests '*CardioLogDraftTest'` first failed 1 of 14: the rower test, whose fixture I had guessed wrong. After fixing the fixture it passed.
- `./gradlew :shared:detekt` passed.
- I did not run the RED step (tests before the code), so there is no recorded compile failure for the revert case.
- I added no pinning tests or old-vs-new diff because the task refactors nothing.

**Guard check**
- I changed the rower cadence range from `10 to 60` to `1 to 250`.
- Result: `rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange` failed and the other 13 passed (`14 tests completed, 1 failed`).
- I restored the file and the run was `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`.

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.math.roundToInt
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private const val MINUTE = 60
+private const val HOUR = 3600
+private const val DAY = 86400
+private const val ROWER_PACE_MAX = 600
+private const val MAX_KM = 1000.0
+private const val SPEED_MAX = 120.0
+private const val SPEED_MIN = 0.1
+private const val SHAPE_ERROR = "use h:mm:ss"
+
+private fun clockText(seconds: Int): String {
+    val s = (seconds % MINUTE).toString().padStart(2, '0')
+    return if (seconds < HOUR) {
+        "${seconds / MINUTE}:$s"
+    } else {
+        "${seconds / HOUR}:${(seconds % HOUR / MINUTE).toString().padStart(2, '0')}:$s"
+    }
+}
+
+private fun parseClock(text: String): Int? {
+    val parts = text.trim().split(":")
+    if (parts.size !in 2..3 || parts.any { it.isEmpty() || !it.all(Char::isDigit) }) return null
+    val nums = parts.map { it.toIntOrNull() ?: return null }
+    if (nums.drop(1).any { it > MINUTE - 1 }) return null
+    return if (nums.size == 2) nums[0] * MINUTE + nums[1] else nums[0] * HOUR + nums[1] * MINUTE + nums[2]
+}
+
+private fun plain(value: Double): String {
+    val text = value.toString()
+    return if (text.endsWith(".0")) text.dropLast(2) else text
+}
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's state (KAN-939): text per field, effort, note and the fields the user has left.
+ * Immutable and pure; the 400 ms debounce lives in the sheet.
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String = texts[field].orEmpty()
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft = copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    private val isRower get() = activity == CardioActivity.ROWER
+    private val distanceUnit get() = if (isRower) DistanceUnit.M else units
+
+    private fun durationSeconds(): Int? = parseClock(text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+
+    private fun distance(): CardioDistance? =
+        text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE).trim().toDoubleOrNull()?.let { CardioDistance(it, distanceUnit) }
+
+    private fun paceSeconds(): Int? {
+        val raw = text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE).trim()
+        return if (raw.count { it == ':' } == 1) parseClock(raw) else null
+    }
+
+    private fun speed(): Double? = text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED).trim().toDoubleOrNull()
+
+    private fun int(field: CardioLogField): Int? = text(field).trim().toIntOrNull()
+
+    private fun unitName() = if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "mi" else "km"
+
+    private fun intRange(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        min: Int,
+        max: Int,
+        unit: String,
+    ): String? {
+        val raw = text(field).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val n = raw.toIntOrNull()
+        return if (n == null || n < min || n > max) "between $min and $max $unit" else null
+    }
+
+    private fun durationError(): String? {
+        val raw = text(CardioLogField.DURATION).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val s = parseClock(raw) ?: return SHAPE_ERROR
+        return if (s < 1 || s > DAY) "between 0:01 and 24:00:00" else null
+    }
+
+    private fun distanceError(): String? {
+        val raw = text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val max = if (isRower) MAX_KM * 1000 else MAX_KM * DistanceUnit.KM.metres / units.metres
+        val v = raw.toDoubleOrNull()
+        val unit = if (isRower) "m" else unitName()
+        return if (v == null || v <= 0 || v >= max) "between 0 and ${plain((max * 10).roundToInt() / 10.0)} $unit" else null
+    }
+
+    private fun paceError(): String? {
+        val raw = text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val max = if (isRower) ROWER_PACE_MAX else HOUR
+        val unit = if (isRower) "/500 m" else "/${unitName()}"
+        val s = paceSeconds()
+        return if (s == null || s < MINUTE || s > max) "between ${clockText(MINUTE)} and ${clockText(max)} $unit" else null
+    }
+
+    private fun speedError(): String? {
+        val raw = text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED).trim()
+        if (raw.isEmpty()) return null
+        val v = speed()
+        val unit = if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "mph" else "km/h"
+        return if (v == null || v < SPEED_MIN || v > SPEED_MAX) "between $SPEED_MIN and ${SPEED_MAX.toInt()} $unit" else null
+    }
+
+    private fun maxHeartRateError(): String? {
+        intRange(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, HR_MIN, HR_MAX, "bpm")?.let { return it }
+        val max = int(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE)
+        val avg = int(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE)
+        return if (max != null && avg != null && max < avg) "max heart rate is below average" else null
+    }
+
+    /** Field → message for every typed field out of range; an empty field is never in error. */
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> {
+        val cadence = if (isRower) 10 to 60 else 1 to CADENCE_MAX
+        val checks =
+            mapOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION to durationError(),
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE to distanceError(),
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE to paceError(),
+                CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED to speedError(),
+                CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN to
+                    intRange(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, 0, ELEVATION_MAX, if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m"),
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to intRange(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, HR_MIN, HR_MAX, "bpm"),
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE to maxHeartRateError(),
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE to intRange(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, cadence.first, cadence.second, "spm"),
+                CardioLogField.AVG_POWER to intRange(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER, 1, POWER_MAX, "W"),
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES to intRange(CardioLogField.CALORIES, 0, CALORIES_MAX, "kcal"),
+            )
+        return checks.mapNotNull { (field, message) -> message?.let { field to it } }.toMap().filterKeys { it in activity.logFields }
+    }
+
+    val canSubmit: Boolean get() = durationSeconds() != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    /** "6:37" / "31.9" when the field is blank and duration and distance are known; null when typed. */
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                if (field !in activity.logFields || text(field).isNotBlank()) {
+                    null
+                } else {
+                    CardioFormat.derivedPace(durationSeconds(), distance(), null, activity, units)?.let(::clockText)
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                if (field !in activity.logFields || text(field).isNotBlank()) {
+                    null
+                } else {
+                    CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(durationSeconds(), distance(), null, units)?.toString()
+                }
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+
+    /** Typed fields and effort only — never the note, never a derived value. */
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest {
+        val fields = activity.logFields
+
+        fun typed(field: CardioLogField) = if (field in fields) int(field) else null
+        return CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = requireNotNull(durationSeconds()) { "duration must parse before a request" },
+            distance = if (CardioLogField.DISTANCE in fields) distance() else null,
+            avgPaceSeconds = if (CardioLogField.AVG_PACE in fields) paceSeconds() else null,
+            avgSpeed = if (CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED in fields) speed() else null,
+            avgHeartRate = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE),
+            maxHeartRate = typed(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE),
+            calories = typed(CardioLogField.CALORIES),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = typed(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN),
+            avgCadence = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE),
+            avgPower = typed(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER),
+        )
+    }
+
+    companion object {
+        private const val HR_MIN = 30
+        private const val HR_MAX = 250
+        private const val CADENCE_MAX = 250
+        private const val POWER_MAX = 2500
+        private const val CALORIES_MAX = 20000
+        private const val ELEVATION_MAX = 20000
+
+        /** A blank draft; Duration prefills from `startedAt` when in progress, floored to the second. */
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val base = CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val started = workout.startedAt
+            if (workout.status != WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS || started == null) return base
+            val seconds = (now - started).inWholeSeconds.coerceAtLeast(0).toInt()
+            return base.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, clockText(seconds))
+        }
+
+        /** A saved log as text, its effort, and the workout's note; derived values stay blank. */
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val base = CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val log = workout.log ?: return base
+            val target = if (workout.activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else workout.distanceUnit
+            val texts =
+                mapOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION to clockText(log.durationSeconds),
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE to
+                        log.distance?.let { plain(it.value * it.unit.metres / target.metres) },
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_PACE to log.avgPaceSeconds?.let(::clockText),
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED to log.avgSpeed?.let(::plain),
+                    CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN to log.elevationGain?.toString(),
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to log.avgHeartRate?.toString(),
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE to log.maxHeartRate?.toString(),
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE to log.avgCadence?.toString(),
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_POWER to log.avgPower?.toString(),
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES to log.calories?.toString(),
+                ).mapNotNull { (f, v) -> v?.let { f to it } }.toMap()
+            return base.copy(texts = texts, effort = log.effort)
+        }
+    }
+}

+@file:Suppress("ktlint:standard:filename")
+
+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+/** One input on the cardio log sheet (KAN-939 `log-fields-table`). */
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+private val runFields =
+    listOf(
+        CardioLogField.DURATION,
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+        CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+        CardioLogField.NOTE,
+    )
+
+private val rideFields =
+    listOf(
+        CardioLogField.DURATION,
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+        CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+        CardioLogField.NOTE,
+    )
+
+internal fun logFieldsOf(activity: CardioActivity): List<CardioLogField> =
+    when (activity) {
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> {
+            runFields
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            rideFields
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            rideFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> {
+            runFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN - CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ROWER -> {
+            rowerFields
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> {
+            ellipticalFields
+        }
+    }
+
+private val rowerFields =
+    listOf(
+        CardioLogField.DURATION,
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+        CardioLogField.NOTE,
+    )
+
+private val ellipticalFields =
+    listOf(
+        CardioLogField.DURATION,
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+        CardioLogField.NOTE,
+    )

+++ b/shared/src/commonTest/kotlin/com/gymie/shared/cardio/CardioLogDraftTest.kt

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertFalse
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.test.assertTrue
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField as F
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "c1",
+        scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 10, 14),
+        displayName = "Run",
+        notes = notes,
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = activity.kinds.first(),
+        distanceUnit = unit,
+        log = log,
+    )
+
+    private fun draft(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+    ) = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(activity, unit))
+
+    @Test
+    fun eachActivityLogsItsOwnFieldsInFrameOrder() {
+        val common = listOf(F.AVG_HEART_RATE, F.MAX_HEART_RATE)
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(F.DURATION, F.DISTANCE, F.AVG_PACE, F.ELEVATION_GAIN) + common +
+                listOf(F.AVG_CADENCE, F.CALORIES, F.EFFORT, F.NOTE),
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(F.DURATION, F.DISTANCE, F.AVG_SPEED, F.ELEVATION_GAIN) + common +
+                listOf(F.AVG_CADENCE, F.AVG_POWER, F.CALORIES, F.EFFORT, F.NOTE),
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields - F.ELEVATION_GAIN, CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE.logFields)
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(F.DURATION, F.DISTANCE, F.AVG_PACE, F.AVG_POWER) + common +
+                listOf(F.AVG_CADENCE, F.CALORIES, F.EFFORT, F.NOTE),
+            CardioActivity.ROWER.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(F.DURATION, F.DISTANCE, F.AVG_PACE) + common + listOf(F.CALORIES, F.EFFORT, F.NOTE),
+            CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(F.DURATION, F.DISTANCE) + common + listOf(F.AVG_CADENCE, F.CALORIES, F.EFFORT, F.NOTE),
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL.logFields,
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        assertEquals(1992, draft().with(F.DURATION, "33:12").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, draft().with(F.DURATION, "1:12:40").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        listOf("45", "1:75", "abc").forEach {
+            val d = draft().with(F.DURATION, it)
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", d.errors()[F.DURATION], it)
+            assertFalse(d.canSubmit, it)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anEmptyDurationHoldsThePrimary() {
+        val d = draft()
+        assertTrue(d.errors().isEmpty())
+        assertFalse(d.canSubmit)
+        assertTrue(d.with(F.DURATION, "33:12").canSubmit)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val d = draft().with(F.DURATION, "33:12").with(F.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+        assertEquals("6:37", d.derived(F.AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgSpeed)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aTypedPaceReplacesTheDerivedOne() {
+        val d = draft().with(F.DURATION, "33:12").with(F.DISTANCE, "5.02").with(F.AVG_PACE, "6:30")
+        assertNull(d.derived(F.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(390, d.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE).with(F.DURATION, "1:12:40").with(F.DISTANCE, "38.6")
+        assertEquals("31.9", d.derived(F.AVG_SPEED))
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgSpeed)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(F.DURATION, "29:54").with(F.DISTANCE, "7120")
+        assertEquals("2:06", d.derived(F.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(7120.0, DistanceUnit.M), d.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val d = draft().with(F.AVG_HEART_RATE, "148").with(F.MAX_HEART_RATE, "139")
+        assertEquals(mapOf(F.MAX_HEART_RATE to "max heart rate is below average"), d.errors())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(F.AVG_CADENCE, "8")
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", d.errors()[F.AVG_CADENCE])
+        assertNull(draft(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(F.AVG_CADENCE, "24").errors()[F.AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val d = draft().with(F.DURATION, "abc")
+        assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", d.errors()[F.DURATION])
+        assertNull(d.shownError(F.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", d.left(F.DURATION).shownError(F.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val w =
+            workout(
+                status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS,
+                startedAt = Instant.parse("2026-10-14T18:31:00.000Z"),
+            )
+        val d = CardioLogDraft.fresh(w, Instant.parse("2026-10-14T19:12:06.900Z"))
+        assertEquals("41:06", d.text(F.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("", CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(), Instant.parse("2026-10-14T19:12:06Z")).text(F.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val log =
+            CardioLog(
+                durationSeconds = 4360,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgHeartRate = 150,
+                maxHeartRate = 171,
+                calories = 420,
+                effort = 7,
+                elevationGain = 24,
+                avgCadence = 168,
+                derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 869,
+            )
+        val d = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(log = log, notes = "hilly"))
+        assertEquals("1:12:40", d.text(F.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", d.text(F.DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("150", d.text(F.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("171", d.text(F.MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("420", d.text(F.CALORIES))
+        assertEquals("24", d.text(F.ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals("168", d.text(F.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("", d.text(F.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", d.derived(F.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(7, d.effort)
+        assertEquals("hilly", d.note)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val d =
+            draft()
+                .with(F.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(F.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+                .with(F.AVG_HEART_RATE, "150")
+                .with(F.AVG_POWER, "200")
+                .copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioLogRequest(
+                durationSeconds = 1992,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgHeartRate = 150,
+                effort = 6,
+            ),
+            d.toRequest(),
+        )
+    }
+}

**What changed**

- The checks use the backend ranges, except rower cadence, which is 10–60 per `client-ranges`. Duration accepts `m:ss` and `h:mm:ss`.
- `CardioLogDraftTest.kt` (new) has the 14 named tests.

**Commands**
- `./gradlew :shared:ktlintFormat` ran clean.
- `./gradlew :shared:desktopTest --tests '*CardioLogDraftTest'` passed on its first run. I didn't capture a RED run before writing the code, so the RED step is not recorded.

After I restored the range the full run above passed. I didn't save the failure output beyond that summary.

**Report, don't decide**
- **Error text:** your spec gives exact messages only for duration shape, max heart rate and cadence. I wrote the others myself: `between 1:00 and 60:00 /km` for pace, `between 0.1 and 120 km/h` for speed, `between 0 and 1000 km` for distance, and `between 0:01 and 24:00:00` for duration range. Effort has no check. Please confirm or correct these.

- **Rower test figures:** your task says to use "L4's figures" for the rower pace, but I don't have that frame. The test uses 7:30 over 1782 m, which gives 2:06, and 30:00 over 7120 m for the metres distance. Swap in L4's figures if they differ.
- **Test inputs:** the `aSavedLogFillsTheDraft` inputs are mine. I used 4360 s over 5.02 km for the `14:29` case.
- **Committer:** the repo had no git identity, so I committed with a throwaway `-c user.name=claude -c user.email=noreply@anthropic.com`.

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_POWER
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.EFFORT
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.NOTE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.math.roundToLong
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private const val HOUR = 3600
+private const val MAX_DURATION = 86400
+private const val MAX_DISTANCE_KM = 1000.0
+private const val THOUSAND = 1000.0
+
+private val DURATION_SHAPE = Regex("""(?:(\d{1,5}):([0-5]\d)|(\d{1,5}):([0-5]\d):([0-5]\d))""")
+
+/** An integer field's backend range; the rower's cadence is stricter (`client-ranges`). */
+private fun intRange(
+    field: CardioLogField,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+): IntRange =
+    when (field) {
+        AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE -> 30..250
+        CALORIES -> 0..20000
+        ELEVATION_GAIN -> 0..20000
+        AVG_CADENCE -> if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) 10..60 else 1..250
+        AVG_POWER -> 1..2500
+        else -> error("$field is not an integer field")
+    }
+
+private fun unitOf(field: CardioLogField): String =
+    when (field) {
+        AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE -> "bpm"
+        CALORIES -> "kcal"
+        AVG_CADENCE -> "spm"
+        AVG_POWER -> "W"
+        else -> ""
+    }
+
+/** `m:ss` or `h:mm:ss` to whole seconds; null for any other shape. */
+internal fun parseClock(text: String): Int? {
+    val m = DURATION_SHAPE.matchEntire(text.trim()) ?: return null
+    val g = m.groupValues
+    return if (g[1].isNotEmpty()) {
+        g[1].toInt() * 60 + g[2].toInt()
+    } else {
+        g[3].toInt() * HOUR + g[4].toInt() * 60 + g[5].toInt()
+    }
+}
+
+private fun number(value: Double): String =
+    if (value ==
+        value.roundToLong().toDouble()
+    ) {
+        value.roundToLong().toString()
+    } else {
+        value.toString()
+    }
+
+private fun paceText(seconds: Int) = clock(seconds)
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's state (KAN-939): typed text per field, the effort and note, and which fields the
+ * user has left. Errors are always computable; [shownError] holds them back until a field was left.
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    private val distanceUnit get() = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units
+    private val paceBasis get() =
+        if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) {
+            "500 m"
+        } else if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) {
+            "mi"
+        } else {
+            "km"
+        }
+
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            EFFORT -> effort?.toString() ?: ""
+            NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field] ?: ""
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.toIntOrNull())
+            NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    private fun durationSeconds(): Int? = parseClock(text(DURATION))
+
+    private fun distance(): CardioDistance? =
+        text(DISTANCE)
+            .trim()
+            .toDoubleOrNull()
+            ?.takeIf {
+                it > 0
+            }?.let { CardioDistance(it, distanceUnit) }
+
+    private fun paceTyped(): Int? = parseClock(text(AVG_PACE))?.takeIf { text(AVG_PACE).count { c -> c == ':' } == 1 }
+
+    private fun speedTyped(): Double? = text(AVG_SPEED).trim().toDoubleOrNull()
+
+    private fun int(field: CardioLogField): Int? = text(field).trim().toIntOrNull()
+
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> {
+        val out = linkedMapOf<CardioLogField, String>()
+        for (field in activity.logFields) {
+            val message = if (text(field).isBlank()) null else check(field)
+            if (message != null) out[field] = message
+        }
+        return out
+    }
+
+    private fun check(field: CardioLogField): String? =
+        when (field) {
+            DURATION -> {
+                durationError()
+            }
+
+            DISTANCE -> {
+                distanceError()
+            }
+
+            AVG_PACE -> {
+                paceError()
+            }
+
+            AVG_SPEED -> {
+                if (speedTyped()?.let { it in 0.1..120.0 } ==
+                    true
+                ) {
+                    null
+                } else {
+                    "between 0.1 and 120 ${if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "mph" else "km/h"}"
+                }
+            }
+
+            EFFORT, NOTE -> {
+                null
+            }
+
+            MAX_HEART_RATE -> {
+                intError(field) ?: maxBelowAvg()
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                intError(field)
+            }
+        }
+
+    private fun durationError(): String? {
+        val s = durationSeconds() ?: return "use h:mm:ss"
+        return if (s in 1..MAX_DURATION) null else "between 0:01 and 24:00:00"
+    }
+
+    private fun distanceError(): String? {
+        val max = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) MAX_DISTANCE_KM * THOUSAND else MAX_DISTANCE_KM * THOUSAND / units.metres
+        val unit =
+            if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) {
+                "m"
+            } else if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) {
+                "mi"
+            } else {
+                "km"
+            }
+        val v = text(DISTANCE).trim().toDoubleOrNull()
+        return if (v != null && v > 0 && v < max) null else "between 0 and ${number(max)} $unit"
+    }
+
+    private fun paceError(): String? {
+        val max = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) 600 else 3600
+        val s = paceTyped()
+        return if (s != null && s in 60..max) null else "between ${paceText(60)} and ${paceText(max)} /$paceBasis"
+    }
+
+    private fun intError(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        val range = intRange(field, activity)
+        val v = int(field)
+        return if (v != null && v in range) null else "between ${range.first} and ${range.last} ${unitOf(field)}".trimEnd()
+    }
+
+    private fun maxBelowAvg(): String? {
+        val avg = int(AVG_HEART_RATE)
+        val max = int(MAX_HEART_RATE)
+        return if (avg != null && max != null && max < avg) "max heart rate is below average" else null
+    }
+
+    val canSubmit: Boolean get() = durationSeconds() != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    /** The calculated pace or speed shown while the field is blank; null when typed or not computable. */
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (field !in activity.logFields || text(field).isNotBlank()) return null
+        return when (field) {
+            AVG_PACE -> {
+                CardioFormat
+                    .derivedPace(durationSeconds(), distance(), null, activity, units)
+                    ?.let(::paceText)
+            }
+
+            AVG_SPEED -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(durationSeconds(), distance(), null, units)?.toString()
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** Typed fields and the effort; never the note (the workout's) and never a derived value. */
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = checkNotNull(durationSeconds()) { "duration is required" },
+            distance = distance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = paceTyped(),
+            avgSpeed = speedTyped(),
+            avgHeartRate = int(AVG_HEART_RATE),
+            maxHeartRate = int(MAX_HEART_RATE),
+            calories = int(CALORIES),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = int(ELEVATION_GAIN),
+            avgCadence = int(AVG_CADENCE),
+            avgPower = int(AVG_POWER),
+        )
+
+    companion object {
+        /** An empty draft; Duration holds the clock's whole seconds when the workout is in progress. */
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val empty = CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val started = workout.startedAt
+            if (workout.status != WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS || started == null) return empty
+            val seconds = (now - started).inWholeSeconds.toInt()
+            return if (seconds <= 0) empty else empty.with(DURATION, durationText(seconds))
+        }
+
+        /** A saved log as text; derived values stay unset so they recompute. */
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val base = CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val log = workout.log ?: return base
+            val shown = base.distanceUnit
+            val texts =
+                buildMap {
+                    put(DURATION, durationText(log.durationSeconds))
+                    log.distance?.let { d ->
+                        val v =
+                            if (d.unit ==
+                                shown
+                            ) {
+                                d.value
+                            } else {
+                                (d.value * d.unit.metres / shown.metres * THOUSAND).roundToLong() / THOUSAND
+                            }
+                        put(DISTANCE, number(v))
+                    }
+                    log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { put(AVG_PACE, paceText(it)) }
+                    log.avgSpeed?.let { put(AVG_SPEED, number(it)) }
+                    log.elevationGain?.let { put(ELEVATION_GAIN, it.toString()) }
+                    log.avgHeartRate?.let { put(AVG_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+                    log.maxHeartRate?.let { put(MAX_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+                    log.avgCadence?.let { put(AVG_CADENCE, it.toString()) }
+                    log.avgPower?.let { put(AVG_POWER, it.toString()) }
+                    log.calories?.let { put(CALORIES, it.toString()) }
+                }
+            return base.copy(texts = texts, effort = log.effort)
+        }
+
+        private fun durationText(seconds: Int) = if (seconds < HOUR) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)
+    }
+}

+@file:Suppress("ktlint:standard:filename")
+
+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_POWER
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.EFFORT
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.NOTE
+
+/** One input of the log sheet; which ones a sheet shows is [CardioActivity.logFields]. */
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+private val outdoorRun =
+    listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, ELEVATION_GAIN, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE)
+private val outdoorRide =
+    listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_SPEED, ELEVATION_GAIN, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, AVG_POWER, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE)
+
+internal fun logFieldsOf(activity: CardioActivity): List<CardioLogField> =
+    when (activity) {
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> {
+            outdoorRun
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> {
+            outdoorRun.filter { it != ELEVATION_GAIN && it != AVG_CADENCE }
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            outdoorRide
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            outdoorRide.filter { it != ELEVATION_GAIN }
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> {
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE)
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ROWER -> {
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, AVG_POWER, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE)
+        }
+    }

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_PACE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_POWER
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.CALORIES
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DISTANCE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.DURATION
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.EFFORT
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.CardioLogField.NOTE
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertFalse
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.test.assertTrue
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "w",
+        scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 5, 1),
+        displayName = "w",
+        notes = notes,
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = activity.kinds.first(),
+        distanceUnit = unit,
+        log = log,
+    )
+
+    private fun draft(
+        activity: CardioActivity,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+    ) = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(activity, unit))
+
+    @Test
+    fun eachActivityLogsItsOwnFieldsInFrameOrder() {
+        val run = listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, ELEVATION_GAIN, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE)
+        val ride =
+            listOf(
+                DURATION,
+                DISTANCE,
+                AVG_SPEED,
+                ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                AVG_CADENCE,
+                AVG_POWER,
+                CALORIES,
+                EFFORT,
+                NOTE,
+            )
+        assertEquals(run, CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields)
+        assertEquals(ride, CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields)
+        assertEquals(ride - ELEVATION_GAIN, CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE.logFields)
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_PACE, AVG_POWER, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE),
+            CardioActivity.ROWER.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(run - ELEVATION_GAIN - AVG_CADENCE, CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN.logFields)
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(DURATION, DISTANCE, AVG_HEART_RATE, MAX_HEART_RATE, AVG_CADENCE, CALORIES, EFFORT, NOTE),
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL.logFields,
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        assertEquals(1992, draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN).with(DURATION, "33:12").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN).with(DURATION, "1:12:40").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        for (bad in listOf("45", "1:75", "abc")) {
+            val d = draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN).with(DURATION, bad)
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", d.errors()[DURATION], bad)
+            assertFalse(d.canSubmit, bad)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anEmptyDurationHoldsThePrimary() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN)
+        assertTrue(d.errors().isEmpty())
+        assertFalse(d.canSubmit)
+        assertTrue(d.with(DURATION, "30:00").canSubmit)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN).with(DURATION, "33:12").with(DISTANCE, "5.02")
+        assertEquals("6:37", d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM), d.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aTypedPaceReplacesTheDerivedOne() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN).with(DURATION, "33:12").with(DISTANCE, "5.02").with(AVG_PACE, "6:30")
+        assertNull(d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(390, d.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE).with(DURATION, "1:12:40").with(DISTANCE, "38.6")
+        assertEquals("31.9", d.derived(AVG_SPEED))
+        assertNull(d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgSpeed)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(DURATION, "7:30").with(DISTANCE, "1782")
+        assertEquals("2:06", d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+        val km = draft(CardioActivity.ROWER, DistanceUnit.KM).with(DURATION, "30:00").with(DISTANCE, "7120")
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(7120.0, DistanceUnit.M), km.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN).with(AVG_HEART_RATE, "148").with(MAX_HEART_RATE, "139")
+        assertEquals(mapOf(MAX_HEART_RATE to "max heart rate is below average"), d.errors())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(AVG_CADENCE, "8")
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", d.errors()[AVG_CADENCE])
+        assertEquals("between 1 and 250 spm", draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN).with(AVG_CADENCE, "0").errors()[AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val d = draft(CardioActivity.ROWER).with(AVG_CADENCE, "8")
+        assertTrue(AVG_CADENCE in d.errors())
+        assertNull(d.shownError(AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", d.left(AVG_CADENCE).shownError(AVG_CADENCE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val w =
+            workout(
+                CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+                status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS,
+                startedAt = Instant.parse("2026-05-01T18:31:00.000Z"),
+            )
+        assertEquals("41:06", CardioLogDraft.fresh(w, Instant.parse("2026-05-01T19:12:06.900Z")).text(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("1:12:40", CardioLogDraft.fresh(w, Instant.parse("2026-05-01T19:43:40.000Z")).text(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("", CardioLogDraft.fresh(w.copy(status = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED), Instant.parse("2026-05-01T19:12:06Z")).text(DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val log =
+            CardioLog(
+                durationSeconds = 4360,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgHeartRate = 150,
+                maxHeartRate = 170,
+                calories = 700,
+                effort = 7,
+                elevationGain = 40,
+                avgCadence = 170,
+                derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 869,
+            )
+        val d = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN, log = log, notes = "hilly"))
+        assertEquals("1:12:40", d.text(DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", d.text(DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("150", d.text(AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("170", d.text(MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("700", d.text(CALORIES))
+        assertEquals("40", d.text(ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals("170", d.text(AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("7", d.text(EFFORT))
+        assertEquals("hilly", d.text(NOTE))
+        assertEquals("", d.text(AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("14:29", d.derived(AVG_PACE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val d =
+            draft(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE)
+                .with(DURATION, "1:12:40")
+                .with(DISTANCE, "38.6")
+                .with(AVG_POWER, "210")
+                .with(CALORIES, "900")
+                .with(EFFORT, "6")
+                .with(NOTE, "windy")
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioLogRequest(
+                durationSeconds = 4360,
+                distance = CardioDistance(38.6, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgPower = 210,
+                calories = 900,
+                effort = 6,
+            ),
+            d.toRequest(),
+        )
+        assertEquals(6, d.copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly").effort)
+    }
+}
